Agent Assets

Assets for build Agent
dockerfile
# Base image: Ubuntu 22.04 for a modern, stable environment
FROM ubuntu:22.04

# Set non-interactive frontend for package installations
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive

# Install essential build tools, compilers, MPI, and utilities
# - build-essential: A meta-package for gcc, g++, make, etc.
# - gfortran: Fortran compiler, often a dependency for HPC libraries.
# - git: For cloning source code repositories.
# - wget: For downloading tarballs.
# - ca-certificates: For secure HTTPS connections.
# - openmpi-bin & libopenmpi-dev: OpenMPI runtime and development headers.
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    build-essential \
    gfortran \
    git \
    wget \
    ca-certificates \
    openmpi-bin \
    libopenmpi-dev \
    && apt-get clean \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Set a persistent working directory for the build process
WORKDIR /build

# Build and install METIS 4.0.3 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# The OPTFLAGS are required to avoid build errors with modern compilers.
RUN wget --no-check-certificate https://github.com/mfem/tpls/raw/gh-pages/metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && cd metis-4.0.3 \
    && make config prefix=/usr/local/metis \
    && export OPTFLAGS="-Wno-error=implicit-function-declaration" && make -j$(nproc) && make install \
    && cd .. \
    && rm metis-4.0.3.tar.gz

# Build and install hypre 2.28.0 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# Flags are set as per instructions: --disable-fortran and --enable-bigint.
# CFLAGS/CXXFLAGS with -fPIC are added to ensure Position-Independent Code,
# which is good practice for shared libraries.
RUN wget https://github.com/hypre-space/hypre/archive/v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && cd hypre-2.28.0/src \
    && ./configure --prefix=/usr/local/hypre --disable-fortran --enable-bigint CFLAGS='-fPIC' CXXFLAGS='-fPIC' \
    && make -j$(nproc) \
    && make install \
    && cd ../.. \
    && rm v2.28.0.tar.gz

# Build MFEM 4.7 from source
# This is a dependency for Laghos. It is built against the previously installed
# METIS and hypre libraries. We build the parallel (MPI) version.
RUN git clone https://github.com/mfem/mfem.git \
    && cd mfem \
    && git checkout v4.7 \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make parallel -j$(nproc) MFEM_USE_MPI=YES HYPRE_DIR=/usr/local/hypre METIS_DIR=/usr/local/metis

# Build Laghos from source
# This is the target application. It is built against the MFEM source tree.
RUN git clone https://github.com/CEED/Laghos.git \
    && cd Laghos \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make -j$(nproc) MFEM_DIR=../mfem

# Set the final application working directory
WORKDIR /app

# Copy the compiled Laghos executable to a system path and copy required data files
# to the application's working directory.
RUN cp /build/Laghos/laghos /usr/local/bin/ \
    && cp -r /build/Laghos/data/* .

# Clean up the build directory to reduce the final image size
RUN rm -rf /build

# Configure OpenMPI for container environments to prevent common issues
# - btl_tcp_if_exclude: Prevents MPI from using loopback/docker network interfaces for communication.
# - btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism: Avoids a mechanism that can cause issues with shared memory in containers.
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_tcp_if_exclude=lo,docker0
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism=none

# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun

84# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun86# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun
 
# Base image: Ubuntu 22.04 for a modern, stable environment
FROM ubuntu:22.04

# Set non-interactive frontend for package installations
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive

# Install essential build tools, compilers, MPI, and utilities
# - build-essential: A meta-package for gcc, g++, make, etc.
# - gfortran: Fortran compiler, often a dependency for HPC libraries.
# - git: For cloning source code repositories.
# - wget: For downloading tarballs.
# - ca-certificates: For secure HTTPS connections.
# - openmpi-bin & libopenmpi-dev: OpenMPI runtime and development headers.
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    build-essential \
    gfortran \
    git \
    wget \
    ca-certificates \
    openmpi-bin \
    libopenmpi-dev \
    && apt-get clean \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Set a persistent working directory for the build process
WORKDIR /build

# Build and install METIS 4.0.3 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# The OPTFLAGS are required to avoid build errors with modern compilers.
# FIX: Corrected build steps for METIS 4.0.3. Removed invalid 'make config' target
# and passed variables directly to the appropriate make commands.
RUN wget --no-check-certificate https://github.com/mfem/tpls/raw/gh-pages/metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && cd metis-4.0.3 \
    && make -j$(nproc) OPTFLAGS="-Wno-error=implicit-function-declaration" \
    && make install prefix=/usr/local/metis \
    && cd .. \
    && rm -rf metis-4.0.3 metis-4.0.3.tar.gz

# Build and install hypre 2.28.0 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# Flags are set as per instructions: --disable-fortran and --enable-bigint.
# CFLAGS/CXXFLAGS with -fPIC are added to ensure Position-Independent Code,
# which is good practice for shared libraries.
RUN wget https://github.com/hypre-space/hypre/archive/v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && cd hypre-2.28.0/src \
    && ./configure --prefix=/usr/local/hypre --disable-fortran --enable-bigint CFLAGS='-fPIC' CXXFLAGS='-fPIC' \
    && make -j$(nproc) \
    && make install \
    && cd ../.. \
    && rm -rf hypre-2.28.0 v2.28.0.tar.gz

# Build MFEM 4.7 from source
# This is a dependency for Laghos. It is built against the previously installed
# METIS and hypre libraries. We build the parallel (MPI) version.
RUN git clone https://github.com/mfem/mfem.git \
    && cd mfem \
    && git checkout v4.7 \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make parallel -j$(nproc) MFEM_USE_MPI=YES HYPRE_DIR=/usr/local/hypre METIS_DIR=/usr/local/metis

# Build Laghos from source
# This is the target application. It is built against the MFEM source tree.
RUN git clone https://github.com/CEED/Laghos.git \
    && cd Laghos \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make -j$(nproc) MFEM_DIR=../mfem

# Set the final application working directory
WORKDIR /app

# Copy the compiled Laghos executable to a system path and copy required data files
# to the application's working directory.
RUN cp /build/Laghos/laghos /usr/local/bin/ \
    && cp -r /build/Laghos/data/* .

# Clean up the build directory to reduce the final image size
RUN rm -rf /build

# Configure OpenMPI for container environments to prevent common issues
# - btl_tcp_if_exclude: Prevents MPI from using loopback/docker network interfaces for communication.
# - btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism: Avoids a mechanism that can cause issues with shared memory in containers.
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_tcp_if_exclude=lo,docker0
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism=none

# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun

86# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun88# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun
 
# Base image: Ubuntu 22.04 for a modern, stable environment
FROM ubuntu:22.04

# Set non-interactive frontend for package installations
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive

# Install essential build tools, compilers, MPI, and utilities
# - build-essential: A meta-package for gcc, g++, make, etc.
# - gfortran: Fortran compiler, often a dependency for HPC libraries.
# - git: For cloning source code repositories.
# - wget: For downloading tarballs.
# - ca-certificates: For secure HTTPS connections.
# - openmpi-bin & libopenmpi-dev: OpenMPI runtime and development headers.
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    build-essential \
    gfortran \
    git \
    wget \
    ca-certificates \
    openmpi-bin \
    libopenmpi-dev \
    && apt-get clean \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Set a persistent working directory for the build process
WORKDIR /build

# Build and install METIS 4.0.3 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# The OPTFLAGS are required to avoid build errors with modern compilers.
# FIX: Replaced the invalid 'make install' target with manual copy commands
# to place the library and header in the desired prefix location.
RUN wget --no-check-certificate https://github.com/mfem/tpls/raw/gh-pages/metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && cd metis-4.0.3 \
    && make -j$(nproc) OPTFLAGS="-Wno-error=implicit-function-declaration" \
    && mkdir -p /usr/local/metis/lib /usr/local/metis/include \
    && cp libmetis.a /usr/local/metis/lib/ \
    && cp Lib/metis.h /usr/local/metis/include/ \
    && cd .. \
    && rm -rf metis-4.0.3 metis-4.0.3.tar.gz

# Build and install hypre 2.28.0 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# Flags are set as per instructions: --disable-fortran and --enable-bigint.
# CFLAGS/CXXFLAGS with -fPIC are added to ensure Position-Independent Code,
# which is good practice for shared libraries.
RUN wget https://github.com/hypre-space/hypre/archive/v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && cd hypre-2.28.0/src \
    && ./configure --prefix=/usr/local/hypre --disable-fortran --enable-bigint CFLAGS='-fPIC' CXXFLAGS='-fPIC' \
    && make -j$(nproc) \
    && make install \
    && cd ../.. \
    && rm -rf hypre-2.28.0 v2.28.0.tar.gz

# Build MFEM 4.7 from source
# This is a dependency for Laghos. It is built against the previously installed
# METIS and hypre libraries. We build the parallel (MPI) version.
RUN git clone https://github.com/mfem/mfem.git \
    && cd mfem \
    && git checkout v4.7 \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make parallel -j$(nproc) MFEM_USE_MPI=YES HYPRE_DIR=/usr/local/hypre METIS_DIR=/usr/local/metis

# Build Laghos from source
# This is the target application. It is built against the MFEM source tree.
RUN git clone https://github.com/CEED/Laghos.git \
    && cd Laghos \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make -j$(nproc) MFEM_DIR=../mfem

# Set the final application working directory
WORKDIR /app

# Copy the compiled Laghos executable to a system path and copy required data files
# to the application's working directory.
RUN cp /build/Laghos/laghos /usr/local/bin/ \
    && cp -r /build/Laghos/data/* .

# Clean up the build directory to reduce the final image size
RUN rm -rf /build

# Configure OpenMPI for container environments to prevent common issues
# - btl_tcp_if_exclude: Prevents MPI from using loopback/docker network interfaces for communication.
# - btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism: Avoids a mechanism that can cause issues with shared memory in containers.
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_tcp_if_exclude=lo,docker0
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism=none

# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun

88# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun89# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun
 
# Base image: Ubuntu 22.04 for a modern, stable environment
FROM ubuntu:22.04

# Set non-interactive frontend for package installations
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive

# Install essential build tools, compilers, MPI, and utilities
# - build-essential: A meta-package for gcc, g++, make, etc.
# - gfortran: Fortran compiler, often a dependency for HPC libraries.
# - git: For cloning source code repositories.
# - wget: For downloading tarballs.
# - ca-certificates: For secure HTTPS connections.
# - openmpi-bin & libopenmpi-dev: OpenMPI runtime and development headers.
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    build-essential \
    gfortran \
    git \
    wget \
    ca-certificates \
    openmpi-bin \
    libopenmpi-dev \
    && apt-get clean \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Set a persistent working directory for the build process
WORKDIR /build

# Build and install METIS 4.0.3 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# The OPTFLAGS are required to avoid build errors with modern compilers.
# This version of METIS lacks a 'make install' target, requiring manual installation.
RUN wget --no-check-certificate https://github.com/mfem/tpls/raw/gh-pages/metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && cd metis-4.0.3 \
    && make -j$(nproc) OPTFLAGS="-Wno-error=implicit-function-declaration" \
    && mkdir -p /usr/local/metis/lib /usr/local/metis/include \
    && cp libmetis.a /usr/local/metis/lib/ \
    && cp Lib/metis.h /usr/local/metis/include/ \
    && cd .. \
    && rm -rf metis-4.0.3 metis-4.0.3.tar.gz

# Build and install hypre 2.28.0 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# Flags are set as per instructions: --disable-fortran and --enable-bigint.
# CFLAGS/CXXFLAGS with -fPIC are added to ensure Position-Independent Code,
# which is good practice for shared libraries.
RUN wget https://github.com/hypre-space/hypre/archive/v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && cd hypre-2.28.0/src \
    && ./configure --prefix=/usr/local/hypre --disable-fortran --enable-bigint CFLAGS='-fPIC' CXXFLAGS='-fPIC' \
    && make -j$(nproc) \
    && make install \
    && cd ../.. \
    && rm -rf hypre-2.28.0 v2.28.0.tar.gz

# Build MFEM 4.7 from source
# This is a dependency for Laghos. It is built against the previously installed
# METIS and hypre libraries. We build the parallel (MPI) version.
RUN git clone https://github.com/mfem/mfem.git \
    && cd mfem \
    && git checkout v4.7 \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make parallel -j$(nproc) MFEM_USE_MPI=YES HYPRE_DIR=/usr/local/hypre METIS_DIR=/usr/local/metis

# Build Laghos from source
# This is the target application. It is built against the MFEM source tree.
# FIX: Override METIS_LIB to provide the correct library path to the linker,
# working around an issue in the Laghos makefile that omits the '/lib' subdirectory.
RUN git clone https://github.com/CEED/Laghos.git \
    && cd Laghos \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make -j$(nproc) MFEM_DIR=../mfem METIS_LIB="-L/usr/local/metis/lib -lmetis"

# Set the final application working directory
WORKDIR /app

# Copy the compiled Laghos executable to a system path and copy required data files
# to the application's working directory.
RUN cp /build/Laghos/laghos /usr/local/bin/ \
    && cp -r /build/Laghos/data/* .

# Clean up the build directory to reduce the final image size
RUN rm -rf /build

# Configure OpenMPI for container environments to prevent common issues
# - btl_tcp_if_exclude: Prevents MPI from using loopback/docker network interfaces for communication.
# - btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism: Avoids a mechanism that can cause issues with shared memory in containers.
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_tcp_if_exclude=lo,docker0
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism=none

# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun

# Base image: Ubuntu 22.04 for a modern, stable environment
FROM ubuntu:22.04

# Set non-interactive frontend for package installations
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive

# Install essential build tools, compilers, MPI, and utilities
# - build-essential: A meta-package for gcc, g++, make, etc.
# - gfortran: Fortran compiler, often a dependency for HPC libraries.
# - git: For cloning source code repositories.
# - wget: For downloading tarballs.
# - ca-certificates: For secure HTTPS connections.
# - openmpi-bin & libopenmpi-dev: OpenMPI runtime and development headers.
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    build-essential \
    gfortran \
    git \
    wget \
    ca-certificates \
    openmpi-bin \
    libopenmpi-dev \
    && apt-get clean \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Set a persistent working directory for the build process
WORKDIR /build

# Build and install METIS 4.0.3 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# The OPTFLAGS are required to avoid build errors with modern compilers.
# This version of METIS lacks a 'make install' target, requiring manual installation.
RUN wget --no-check-certificate https://github.com/mfem/tpls/raw/gh-pages/metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf metis-4.0.3.tar.gz \
    && cd metis-4.0.3 \
    && make -j$(nproc) OPTFLAGS="-Wno-error=implicit-function-declaration" \
    && mkdir -p /usr/local/metis/lib /usr/local/metis/include \
    && cp libmetis.a /usr/local/metis/lib/ \
    && cp Lib/metis.h /usr/local/metis/include/ \
    && cd .. \
    && rm -rf metis-4.0.3 metis-4.0.3.tar.gz

# Build and install hypre 2.28.0 from source
# This is a dependency for MFEM.
# Flags are set as per instructions: --disable-fortran and --enable-bigint.
# CFLAGS/CXXFLAGS with -fPIC are added to ensure Position-Independent Code,
# which is good practice for shared libraries.
RUN wget https://github.com/hypre-space/hypre/archive/v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && tar -xzvf v2.28.0.tar.gz \
    && cd hypre-2.28.0/src \
    && ./configure --prefix=/usr/local/hypre --disable-fortran --enable-bigint CFLAGS='-fPIC' CXXFLAGS='-fPIC' \
    && make -j$(nproc) \
    && make install \
    && cd ../.. \
    && rm -rf hypre-2.28.0 v2.28.0.tar.gz

# Build MFEM 4.7 from source
# This is a dependency for Laghos. It is built against the previously installed
# METIS and hypre libraries. We build the parallel (MPI) version.
# FIX: The MFEM makefile's auto-detection for METIS fails to find the library in the '/lib'
# subdirectory. We explicitly provide the correct METIS_LIB flags to ensure they are
# baked into MFEM's configuration, which Laghos will then inherit correctly.
RUN git clone https://github.com/mfem/mfem.git \
    && cd mfem \
    && git checkout v4.7 \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make parallel -j$(nproc) MFEM_USE_MPI=YES HYPRE_DIR=/usr/local/hypre METIS_DIR=/usr/local/metis METIS_LIB="-L/usr/local/metis/lib -lmetis"

# Build Laghos from source
# This is the target application. It is built against the MFEM source tree.
# The necessary library paths are correctly inherited from the fixed MFEM build configuration.
RUN git clone https://github.com/CEED/Laghos.git \
    && cd Laghos \
    && export CXX=mpicxx CC=mpicc && make -j$(nproc) MFEM_DIR=../mfem

# Set the final application working directory
WORKDIR /app

# Copy the compiled Laghos executable to a system path and copy required data files
# to the application's working directory.
RUN cp /build/Laghos/laghos /usr/local/bin/ \
    && cp -r /build/Laghos/data/* .

# Clean up the build directory to reduce the final image size
RUN rm -rf /build

# Configure OpenMPI for container environments to prevent common issues
# - btl_tcp_if_exclude: Prevents MPI from using loopback/docker network interfaces for communication.
# - btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism: Avoids a mechanism that can cause issues with shared memory in containers.
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_tcp_if_exclude=lo,docker0
ENV OMPI_MCA_btl_vader_single_copy_mechanism=none

# The container is now ready to run the 'laghos' executable via mpirun
